============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& One \"stepper\" thread walks the stream once\n    // (advancing the engine with discard(), which is ~300x cheaper than the\n    // form factor work per pair) and publishes the RNG state at the start of\n    // each chunk into a ring buffer. All threads then pull chunks dynamically\n    // from the ring, copy the published state and compute the chunk's form\n    // factors. This gives dynamic load balancing with a single stream walk.\n    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t chunkPairs = std::max<size_t>(\n        4, totalPairs / (static_cast<size_t>(maxThreads) * 32));\n    const size_t numChunks = (totalPairs + chunkPairs - 1) / chunkPairs;\n    const size_t ringSize = std::max<size_t>(64, 4 * static_cast<size_t>(maxThreads));\n\n    struct alignas(64) Slot {\n        std::atomic<int64_t> ready{-1};   // chunk id whose start state is stored here\n        std::atomic<int64_t> taken{-1};   // chunk id whose state has been copied out\n    };\n    std::vector<Slot> slots(ringSize);\n    std::vector<RandomGenerator> ringStates(ringSize);\n    alignas(64) std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n\n    // Number of rand() calls consumed by chunk c in the sequential version\n    auto chunkRands = [&](size_t c) -> uint64_t {\n        const size_t k0 = c * chunkPairs;\n        const size_t k1 = std::min(totalPairs, k0 + chunkPairs);\n        uint64_t count = 0;\n        size_t i = k0 / n;\n        size_t j = k0 % n;\n        for (size_t k = k0; The pair index space is split into chunks; one \"stepper\" thread walks the stream once with `discard()` (about 300x cheaper than the form-factor work) and publishes RNG snapshots at chunk starts into a lock-free ring buffer. All threads pull chunks dynamically. This yields bit-identical results, a single stream walk, and dynamic load balancing. Unbound full-occupancy runs paid 10 to 15 ms per parallel region on this 2-socket box.
